00009: function isISODate(s) {
00010:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00011:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00012: 
00013:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00014:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00015: 
00016:   return (
00017:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
00018:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
00019:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
00020:   );
00021: }
00022: 
00023: function ymdInTimeZone(date, timeZone = "America/Sao_Paulo") {
00024:   if (!(date instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return null;
00025: 
00026:   try {
00027:     const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
00028:       timeZone,
00029:       year: "numeric",
00030:       month: "2-digit",
00031:       day: "2-digit",
00032:     }).formatToParts(date);
00033: 
00034:     const y = parts.find((p) => p.type === "year")?.value;
00035:     const m = parts.find((p) => p.type === "month")?.value;
00036:     const d = parts.find((p) => p.type === "day")?.value;
00037: 
00038:     if (!y || !m || !d) return null;
00039:     const out = `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
00040:     return isISODate(out) ? out : null;
00041:   } catch {
00042:     // fallback local timezone do servidor (menos ideal)
00043:     const y = date.getFullYear();
00044:     const m = pad2(date.getMonth() + 1);
00045:     const d = pad2(date.getDate());
00046:     const out = `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
00047:     return isISODate(out) ? out : null;

00125:   return (
00126:     code.includes("failed-precondition") ||

00128:     (msg.includes("index") && msg.includes("create")) ||
00129:     (msg.includes("requires") && msg.includes("index"))

00133: async function tryBoundsByField(db, lotteryKey, field, scanLimit = 50) {
00134:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00135:   const base = db.collection("draws").where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey);
00136: 
00137:   const ascSnap = await base
00138:     .orderBy(field, "asc")
00139:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc")
00140:     .limit(scanLimit)
00141:     .get();
00142: 
00143:   const descSnap = await base
00144:     .orderBy(field, "desc")
00145:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "desc")
00146:     .limit(scanLimit)
00147:     .get();
00148: 
00149:   if (ascSnap.empty || descSnap.empty) {
00150:     return {
00151:       ok: false,
00152:       source: `where(lottery_key)+orderBy(${field})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00153:     };
00154:   }
00155: 
00156:   function pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(docs, fieldName) {
00157:     for (const doc of docs || []) {
00158:       const v = doc?.data()?.[fieldName];
00159:       const y = normalizeToYMD(v);
00160:       if (y && isISODate(y)) {
00161:         return { ymd: y, docId: doc.id, raw: v };
00162:       }
00163:     }
00164:     return { ymd: null, docId: null, raw: null };
00165:   }
00166: 
00167:   const minPick = pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(ascSnap.docs, field);
00168:   const maxPick = pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(descSnap.docs, field);
00169: 
00170:   return {
00171:     ok: !!(minPick.ymd && maxPick.ymd),
00172:     minYmd: minPick.ymd,
00173:     maxYmd: maxPick.ymd,
00174:     source: `where(lottery_key)+orderBy(${field})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00175:     minDocId: minPick.docId,
00176:     maxDocId: maxPick.docId,
00177:     minRaw: minPick.raw ?? null,
00178:     maxRaw: maxPick.raw ?? null,
00179:   };
00180: }
00181: 
00182: async function fallbackBoundsByDocIdEdges(db, lotteryKey, edgeLimit) {
00183:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00184:   const base = db.collection("draws").where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey);
00185: 
00186:   const ascSnap = await base.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc").limit(edgeLimit).get();
00187:   const descSnap = await base.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc").limitToLast(edgeLimit).get();
00188: 
00189:   const merged = [...ascSnap.docs, ...descSnap.docs];
00190:   const { minYmd, maxYmd } = pickMinMaxFromDocs(merged);
00191: 
00192:   return {
00193:     ok: !!(minYmd && maxYmd),
00194:     minYmd: minYmd || null,
00195:     maxYmd: maxYmd || null,
00196:     source: `fallback_edges_docId(limit=${edgeLimit})`,
00197:     sampleCount: merged.length,
00198:     firstDocId: ascSnap.docs[0]?.id || null,
00199:     lastDocId: descSnap.docs[descSnap.docs.length - 1]?.id || null,
00200:   };
00201: }

00109:  * - Busca N docs DESC e pega o primeiro que vira ISO válido

00112: async function tryBoundsByField(db, lotteryKey, field, scanLimit = 50) {
00113:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00114: 
00115:   const base = db.collection("draws").where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey);
00116: 
00117:   const ascSnap = await base
00118:     .orderBy(field, "asc")
00119:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc")
00120:     .limit(scanLimit)
00121:     .get();
00122: 
00123:   const descSnap = await base
00124:     .orderBy(field, "desc")
00125:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "desc")
00126:     .limit(scanLimit)
00127:     .get();
00128: 
00129:   if (ascSnap.empty || descSnap.empty) {
00130:     return {
00131:       ok: false,
00132:       source: `where(lottery_key)+orderBy(${field})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00133:     };
00134:   }
00135: 
00136:   function pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(docs, fieldName) {
00137:     for (const doc of docs || []) {
00138:       const v = doc?.data()?.[fieldName];
00139:       const y = normalizeToYMD(v);
00140:       if (y && isISODate(y)) {
00141:         return { ymd: y, docId: doc.id, raw: v };
00142:       }
00143:     }
00144:     return { ymd: null, docId: null, raw: null };
00145:   }
00146: 
00147:   const minPick = pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(ascSnap.docs, field);
00148:   const maxPick = pickFirstValidYmdFromDocs(descSnap.docs, field);
00149: 
00150:   return {
00151:     ok: !!(minPick.ymd && maxPick.ymd),
00152:     minYmd: minPick.ymd,
00153:     maxYmd: maxPick.ymd,
00154:     source: `where(lottery_key)+orderBy(${field})+docId(scan=${scanLimit})`,
00155:     minDocId: minPick.docId,
00156:     maxDocId: maxPick.docId,
00157:     minRaw: minPick.raw ?? null,
00158:     maxRaw: maxPick.raw ?? null,
00159:   };
00160: }

00162: async function fallbackBoundsByDocIdEdges(db, lotteryKey, edgeLimit) {
00163:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00164:   const base = db.collection("draws").where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey);
00165: 
00166:   const ascSnap = await base.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc").limit(edgeLimit).get();
00167:   const descSnap = await base.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc").limitToLast(edgeLimit).get();
00168: 
00169:   const merged = [...ascSnap.docs, ...descSnap.docs];
00170:   const { minYmd, maxYmd } = pickMinMaxFromDocs(merged);

00172:   return {
00173:     ok: !!(minYmd && maxYmd),

00001: "use strict";

00005: function pad2(n) { return String(n).padStart(2, "0"); }
00006: 
00007: /**
00008:  * Formata Date -> YYYY-MM-DD no timezone informado (padrão: America/Sao_Paulo).
00009:  * Evita “virada de dia” quando o servidor roda em UTC mas o dado é local (BRT).
00010:  */
00011: function formatDateToYMDInTZ(date, timeZone = "America/Sao_Paulo") {
00012:   if (!(date instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return null;
00013: 
00014:   // formatToParts => { year, month, day } no TZ pedido
00015:   const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
00016:     timeZone,
00017:     year: "numeric",
00018:     month: "2-digit",
00019:     day: "2-digit",
00020:   }).formatToParts(date);
00021: 
00022:   const map = Object.fromEntries(parts.map((p) => [p.type, p.value]));
00023:   const y = map.year, m = map.month, d = map.day;
00024:   if (!y || !m || !d) return null;
00025:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
00026: }
00027: 
00028: function isISODateStrict(s) {
00029:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00030:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00031:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00032:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00033:   return dt.getUTCFullYear() === y && dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 && dt.getUTCDate() === d;
00034: }
00035:

00037:   if (!input) return null;

00039:   // Firestore Timestamp (admin.firestore.Timestamp)
00040:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
00041:     const d = input.toDate();
00042:     const ymd = formatDateToYMDInTZ(d, timeZone);
00043:     if (ymd) return ymd;
00044:   }
00045: 
00046:   // objetos {seconds} / {_seconds}
00047:   if (

00155: async function fetchRowsForRange(db, { lotteryKey, fromYmd, toYmd, pageSize = 2000, maxPages = 80 } = {}) {
00156:   const lot = lotteryKey ? String(lotteryKey).trim().toUpperCase() : null;
00157: 
00158:   // 1) tentativa indexada (aceita range parcial)
00159:   try {
00160:     let q = db.collection("draws");
00161:     if (lot) q = q.where("lottery_key", "==", lot);
00162:     if (fromYmd) q = q.where("ymd", ">=", fromYmd);
00163:     if (toYmd) q = q.where("ymd", "<=", toYmd);
00164: 
00165:     const snap = await q.get();
00166:     const rows = snap.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...(d.data() || {}) }));
00167:     return { rows, source: "query: where(lottery_key)+where(ymd>=/<=)", usedFallback: false };
00168:   } catch (e) {
00169:     if (!isIndexError(e)) throw e;
00170:   }
00171: 
00172:   // 2) fallback sem índice: pagina por docId e filtra em memória
00173:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00174:   let q = db.collection("draws");
00175:   if (lot) q = q.where("lottery_key", "==", lot);
00176:   q = q.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc");
00177: 
00178:   let last = null;
00179:   const out = [];
00180: 
00181:   for (let page = 1; page <= maxPages; page++) {
00182:     let qq = q.limit(pageSize);
00183:     if (last) qq = qq.startAfter(last);
00184: 
00185:     const snap = await qq.get();
00186:     if (snap.empty) break;
00187: 
00188:     for (const doc of snap.docs) {
00189:       const data = doc.data() || {};
00190:       const ymd = normalizeToYMD(data.ymd ?? data.date ?? data.draw_date ?? data.close_date ?? data.dt ?? data.data);
00191:       if (!ymd || !isISODateStrict(ymd)) continue;
00192:       if (fromYmd && ymd < fromYmd) continue;
00193:       if (toYmd && ymd > toYmd) continue;
00194:       out.push({ id: doc.id, ...data });
00195:     }
00196: 
00197:     last = snap.docs[snap.docs.length - 1];
00198:   }
00199: 
00200:   return { rows: out, source: "fallback: paginate by docId + filter(ymd)", usedFallback: true };
00201: }
00202: 
00203: function parseArg(name, def = null) {
00204:   const i = process.argv.findIndex((x) => x === name);
00205:   if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1] != null) return process.argv[i + 1];
00206:   return def;
00207: }
00208: 
00209: async function main() {
00210:   const db = getDb();
00211: 
00212:   const lot = parseArg("--lot", "PT_RIO");
00213:   const fromArg = parseArg("--from", null);
00214:   const toArg = parseArg("--to", null);
00215: 
00216:   const pageSize = Math.max(200, Number(parseArg("--page", "2000")) || 2000);
00217:   const maxPages = Math.max(1, Number(parseArg("--maxPages", "120")) || 120);

00221:   const fromYmd = fromArg && isISODateStrict(fromArg) ? fromArg : null;
00222:   const toYmd = toArg && isISODateStrict(toArg) ? toArg : null;
00223: 
00224:   const fr = await fetchRowsForRange(db, { lotteryKey, fromYmd, toYmd, pageSize, maxPages });
00225:   const audit = auditCountsFromDocs(fr.rows);

00230:   console.log(`fetch_source: ${fr.source}${fr.usedFallback ? " (NO-INDEX fallback)" : ""}`);
00231:   console.log("----------------------------------");

00022: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00023:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00024:   if (/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(s)) return s;
00025: 
00026:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00027:   if (m1) return `${String(m1[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00028: 
00029:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00030:   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00031: 
00032:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00033:   if (m3) return `${String(m3[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(m3[2]).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00034: 
00035:   return "";
00036: }
00037:

00063: async function reconcileDay({ db, ymd, lotteryKey, fix }) {
00064:   const payload = await fetchKingResults({ date: ymd, lotteryKey });
00065:   const sourceDraws = unwrapDraws(payload);
00066: 
00067:   const sourceByHour = new Map();
00068: 
00069:   for (const draw of sourceDraws) {
00070:     const hour = normHour(draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora);
00071:     const prizes = countSourcePrizes(draw);
00072:     if (hour && prizes >= 7) {
00073:       sourceByHour.set(hour, { hour, prizes });
00074:     }
00075:   }
00076: 
00077:   const firestoreByHour = await readFirestoreDay(db, ymd, lotteryKey);
00078: 
00079:   const missing = [];
00080:   const incomplete = [];
00081: 
00082:   for (const [hour, src] of sourceByHour.entries()) {
00083:     const dbRow = firestoreByHour.get(hour);
00084: 
00085:     if (!dbRow) {
00086:       missing.push(hour);
00087:     } else if (dbRow.prizes < src.prizes) {
00088:       incomplete.push({ hour, firestorePrizes: dbRow.prizes, sourcePrizes: src.prizes });
00089:     }
00090:   }
00091: 
00092:   const needsFix = missing.length > 0 || incomplete.length > 0;
00093: 
00094:   let fixed = false;
00095: 
00096:   if (needsFix && fix) {
00097:     await runImport({ date: ymd, lotteryKey });
00098:     fixed = true;
00099: 
00100:     const afterFirestoreByHour = await readFirestoreDay(db, ymd, lotteryKey);
00101:     const afterMissing = [];
00102:     const afterIncomplete = [];
00103: 
00104:     for (const [hour, src] of sourceByHour.entries()) {
00105:       const dbRow = afterFirestoreByHour.get(hour);
00106: 
00107:       if (!dbRow) {
00108:         afterMissing.push(hour);
00109:       } else if (dbRow.prizes < src.prizes) {
00110:         afterIncomplete.push({
00111:           hour,
00112:           firestorePrizes: dbRow.prizes,
00113:           sourcePrizes: src.prizes,
00114:         });
00115:       }
00116:     }
00117: 
00118:     const stillNeedsFix = afterMissing.length > 0 || afterIncomplete.length > 0;
00119: 
00120:     return {
00121:       ymd,
00122:       sourceHours: Array.from(sourceByHour.keys()).sort(),
00123:       firestoreHours: Array.from(afterFirestoreByHour.keys()).sort(),
00124:       missing: afterMissing,
00125:       incomplete: afterIncomplete,
00126:       status: stillNeedsFix ? "FIX_FAILED" : "FIXED",
00127:       fixed,
00128:     };
00129:   }
00130: 
00131:   return {
00132:     ymd,
00133:     sourceHours: Array.from(sourceByHour.keys()).sort(),
00134:     firestoreHours: Array.from(firestoreByHour.keys()).sort(),
00135:     missing,
00136:     incomplete,
00137:     status: needsFix ? "NEEDS_FIX" : "OK",
00138:     fixed,
00139:   };
00140: }
00141:

00020: function ts() {
00021:   const d = new Date();
00022:   const yyyy = d.getFullYear();
00023:   const mm = pad2(d.getMonth() + 1);
00024:   const dd = pad2(d.getDate());
00025:   const hh = pad2(d.getHours());
00026:   const mi = pad2(d.getMinutes());
00027:   const ss = pad2(d.getSeconds());
00028:   return `${yyyy}${mm}${dd}-${hh}${mi}${ss}`;
00029: }

00025: function isValidYMD(ymd) {
00026:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00027:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s)) return false;
00028: 
00029:   const [Y, M, D] = s.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
00030:   if (!Number.isFinite(Y) || !Number.isFinite(M) || !Number.isFinite(D)) return false;
00031:   if (M < 1 || M > 12 || D < 1 || D > 31) return false;
00032: 
00033:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D));
00034:   return (
00035:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === Y &&
00036:     dt.getUTCMonth() + 1 === M &&
00037:     dt.getUTCDate() === D
00038:   );
00039: }
00040:

00042:  * Converte Date -> YYYY-MM-DD considerando timezone fixo (SP).

00044:  */

00046:   if (!(d instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;

00052:       month: "2-digit",
00053:       day: "2-digit",
00054:     });
00055: 
00056:     const parts = fmt.formatToParts(d);
00057:     const y = parts.find((p) => p.type === "year")?.value;
00058:     const m = parts.find((p) => p.type === "month")?.value;
00059:     const da = parts.find((p) => p.type === "day")?.value;
00060: 
00061:     const ymd = `${y}-${m}-${da}`;
00062:     return isValidYMD(ymd) ? ymd : null;
00063:   } catch {

00065:     const m = String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
